Title: Runtime | Case Manager
============================================================

On this page  
  
# Runtime

Deprecated API specification

Case Manager is currently moving to an API specification generated with Swagger. Due to Case Manager's API size and complexity, the resources are being gradually moved to the new format. Until this transition is complete, please make sure that you check both this page and the [new API specification](/case-manager/13.17/api/).

When all resources are moved to the new format, this page will be deleted.

This page details the REST API regarding Runtime.

### Eventâ

**Get the Event matching the given ID**

**Request**

Method| Endpoint| Payload| Parameters| Returns| Description  
---|---|---|---|---|---  
**GET**| 
    
    
    /api/event/<id>

| \--| \--| Event as a JSON object| Get the Event matching the given ID  
**Example****Request**| `/api/event/511a046b-58d9-402b-9559*-c5d993cc17a9`

**Get a paginated list of Events.**

**Request**

Method| Endpoint| Payload| Parameters| Returns| Description  
---|---|---|---|---|---  
**GET**| 
    
    
    /api/event?<pagination parameters>

| \--| Pagination| A paginated list of Events as a JSON object| Get a paginated list of Events.  
**Example****Request**| `/api/event?limit=10*&offset=0`  
---|---

**Get a paginated list of Events for Omnichannel**

**Request**

Method| Endpoint| Payload| Parameters| Returns| Description  
---|---|---|---|---|---  
POST| 
    
    
    /api/event

| \--| Pagination| A paginated list of Events from a given channel as a JSON object| Get a paginated list of Events for a given channnel  
**Example** Request| **channel search on common fields**
    
    
     /api/event
    
    	{
    
    	 "filters": {
    
    	 "COMMON": "'customer_id' gteq '1405378800000'"
    
    	 },
    
    	 "limit": 10,
    
    	 "offset": 0,
    
    	 "totalThreshold": 1400,
    
    	 "orderBy": "timestamp asc"
    
    }

**Omnichannel search on cmp channel fields**
    
    
    /api/event
    
    	{
    
    	"filters": {
    
    	"cmp": "'schema.fields.chnl_type' eq 'CC'"
    
    	},
    
    	"limit": 10,
    
    	"offset": 0,
    
    	"totalThreshold": 1400,
    
    	"orderBy": "timestamp asc"
    
    }

**Omnichannel search on common and cmp channel fields**
    
    
     /api/event
    
    	{
    
    	"filters": {
    
    	"COMMON": "'customer_id' gteq '1405378800000'",
    
    	"cmp": "schema.fields.chnl_type' eq 'CC'"
    
    	},
    
    	"limit": 10,
    
    	"offset": 0,
    
    	"totalThreshold": 1400,
    
    	"orderBy": "timestamp asc"
    
    }  
  
---|---

**Get the next event to be reviewed by a given user**

**Request**

Method| Endpoint| Payload| Parameters| Returns| Description  
---|---|---|---|---|---  
GET| 
    
    
    /api/event/review/next

| \--| \--| An Event model with the event that was assigned to the given user, in case one was assigned.| Get the next event to be reviewed by a given user.  
**Example** Request| `/api/event/review/next`  
---|---

**Get a list of linked events**

**Request**

Method| Endpoint| Payload| Parameters| Returns| Description  
---|---|---|---|---|---  
**GET**| 
    
    
    /api/event/<id>/linked?<pagination parameters>

| \--| Pagination| A paginated list of Events as a JSON object| Get a paginated list of Linked Events to the given event  
**Example****Request**| `/api/event/511a046b-58d9-402b-9559*-c5d993cc17a9/linked?limit=10*&offset=0`

### Event Statusâ

**Change the Status of a set of Events.**

**Request**

Method| Endpoint| Payload| Parameters| Returns| Description  
---|---|---|---|---|---  
**POST**| 
    
    
    /api/event-status

| 
    
    
    {
    	"ids": [
    
    	{
    
    	"id": "0ca69e57-ceda-4ce3-a945-bed5a6c3ea33",
    
    	"timestamp": 1534760171590
    
    	}
    
    	],
    
    	"statusId": "fraud",
    
    	"statusReasonIds": [
    
    	"5e7f91a1-dcfc-47a7-ab69-86819a75b411",
    
    	"ab678a03-99b5-4d2e-8374-66ad619c0719"
    
    	],
    
    	"note": "note"
    
    }

| \--| \--| Change the Status of a set of Events. The sourceEventId is optional.  
**Example****Request**| **URL request**`/api/event-status`**Payload**
    
    
    {
    	"ids": [
    
    	{
    
    	"id": "0ca69e57-ceda-4ce3-a945-bed5a6c3ea33",
    
    	"timestamp": 1534760171590
    
    	}
    
    	],
    
    	"statusId": "fraud",
    
    	"statusReasonIds": [
    
    	"5e7f91a1-dcfc-47a7-ab69-86819a75b411",
    
    	"ab678a03-99b5-4d2e-8374-66ad619c0719"
    
    	],
    
    	"note": "note"
    
    }  
  
---|---

### Event Userâ

**Request**

**Get the list of available Case Types**

**Request**

Method| Endpoint| Payload| Parameters| Returns| Description  
---|---|---|---|---|---  
GET| 
    
    
    /api/case/case-types

| \--| \--| Lits of available Case Types| 

  * Gets a list of the configured case types that the current user has at least read-level access to see. Each case type entry contains its name, configured source types and default source type.
  * This is a list of i18n keys. Frontend should lookup the translation values for those keys. If translation not available (ie. missing entry at AM_MESSAGE table), the raw key value should be used in the select box (allows CS to bypass the need to extend AM_MESSAGE)

  
**Example** Request| | `/api/case/case-types`  
---  
Response|   
HTTP 200| `[``{`` "id": null,`` "name": "type.fraud",`` "sourceTypes": [`` "case.source.type.1",`` "case.source.type.2",`` "case.source.type.N"`` ],`` "defaultSourceType": "case.source.type.1"`` },``{`` "id": null,`` "name": "type.aml",`` "sourceTypes": [`` "case.source.type.3"`` ],`` "defaultSourceType": null`` },``]`

### Case Enricherâ

**Lists all entities that are associated to case's events, considering given entity and event fields.**

**Request**

Method| Endpoint| Payload| Parameters| Returns| Description  
---|---|---|---|---|---  
GET| 
    
    
    /api/case-enricher//

| \--| \--| A list of entities as a JSON object.| Lists all entities that are associated to case's events, considering given entity and event fields.  
**Example** Request| **URL request**`GET /api/case-enricher/<caseId>/<entity>?timestamp=<caseTs>&eventField=<{eventField::set}>&[channelId=<channelId>]&offset=0&limit=1000`

  * channelId
    * optional parameter, DEFAULT channel Id will be considered
    * specific channelId, request will target the respective channel Id and event field(s) should belong to that channel. This is used to feed channel-specific case widgets.
    * OMNI, request will be on field(s) common to all channels. This is used to feed omni case widgets.

**Payload**
    
    
    {
    	"items": [
    
    	    <ENRICHED_ENTITIES>
    
    	],
    
    	"total": 10,
    
    	"limit": 10,
    
    	"offset": 0,
    
    	"filter": null,
    
    	"orderBy": null,
    
    	"totalThreshold": null
    
    }  
  
---|---  
Response| HTTP 200Â

### Self-Service Rulesâ

Case Manager behaves as a proxy between Pulse's REST API and Case Manager interface, so the domain objects (Self-Service Rule object, Rules Project object, ...) is not defined by Case Manager, Â should see the Pulse documentation to get more information about it.Â 

**Self-service rules' context**

In order to specify in which rules block and tenant, if applicable, the user are working, should send the following query parameters:

Attribute| Description| Example  
---|---|---  
blockId| The Rules Block ID.| GET /api/srr?blockId=1f000beb-e1da-4581-916b-2f483c142257  
tenantId| The Tenant ID, required for Tenanted Rules Block| GET /api/srr?blockId=1f000beb-e1da-4581-916b-2f483c142257&tenantId=gIDjMKcru7r5-63vE5NEeg

Method| Endpoint| Payload| Parameters| Returns| Description  
---|---|---|---|---|---

    /api/ssr/publish?<context parameters>

| \--| Self-service rules' context| Publish the Self-Service Rules for the selected rules block / tenant.| Publish the Self-Service Rules.  
**Example** Request| | `/api/ssr/publish?blockId=1f000beb-e1da-4581-916b-2f483c142257&tenantId=gIDjMKcru7r5-63vE5NEeg`

### Self-Service Rules Testingâ

Case Manager behaves as a proxy between Pulse's REST API and Case Manager interface, so the domain objects (Self-Service Rule Test object, ...) is not defined by Case Manager, Â should see the Pulse documentation to get more information about it.Â 

**Self-service rules testing's context**

In order to specify in which rules block and tenant, if applicable, the user are working, should send the following query parameters:

Attribute| Description| Example  
---|---|---  
blockId| The Rules Block ID.| GET /api/srr-testing/oUjTm4WewGgbfibQ_3lMyQ/latest?blockId=1f000beb-e1da-4581-916b-2f483c142257  
tenantId| The Tenant ID, required for Tenanted Rules Block| GET /api/srr-testing/oUjTm4WewGgbfibQ_3lMyQ/latest?blockId=1f000beb-e1da-4581-916b-2f483c142257&tenantId=gIDjMKcru7r5-63vE5NEeg  
**Create a Self-Service Rule Test**

Request

Method| Endpoint| Payload| Parameters| Returns| Description  
---|---|---|---|---|---  
POST| 
    
    
    /api/ssr-testing/<rule-id>?<context parameters>

| The test configuration.| Self-service rules testing' context and the self-service rule id.| The test model. It'll return the Pulse response so see Pulse documentation to know more about the response.| Create a Self-Service Rule Test.  
  
It supports two different types of test configuration:

  * **Custom** is an interval that receive two dates as string and then converts them to instant objects.
  * **Pre-set** is an interval defined by last x time, eg "last 48 hours", "last week", where you specify the unit and the value to calculate the interval.



Example

Request| **URL request**| `/api/ssr-testing/lM2Cx70J82V8ParNHIdIUQ?blockId=1f000beb-e1da-4581-916b-2f483c142257`  
---  
**Payload - Custom Interval**`{``Â Â Â Â "dateType": "CUSTOM",` `Â Â Â Â "from": "2018-06-01T11:00:00Z", // only supports dates in the format "yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ssZ" *` `Â Â Â Â "to": "2018-06-03T11:00:00Z"`Â Â Â `// only supports dates in the format "yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ssZ"``}`  
---  
**Payload - Pre-set Interval**`{``Â Â Â Â "dateType": "PRE_SET",``Â Â Â Â "unit": "days", // only supports ["days", "hours", "minutes"]*``Â Â Â Â "value": 1``}`  
---  
Response| HTTP 200Â | `{``Â Â Â Â "config": {``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "amountFieldName": "amount",``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "fromTs": 1527850800,``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "targetType": "RULE",``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "toTs": ### Custom Entity Exportâ

**Export a custom entity table**

**Request**

Method| Endpoint| Payload| Parameters| Returns| Description  
---|---|---|---|---|---  
**POST**| 
    
    
    /api/entity/<entityName>/export

| **Payload**
    
    
    {
    	"entityName": <name of the entity>
    
    	    "exportFileType": <"CSV" or "XLSX">,
    
    	    "limit": <entries to export limit",
    
    	    "locale": <locale>,
    
    	    "filename": <filename>,
    
    	    "separator": <separator necessary in case of CSV type>,
    
    	    "filenameDateFormat": "dd-MM-yyyy HH'h'mm'm'ss's'",
    
    	    "columnModels": [
    
    	        {
    
    	            "title": <title to show up in export for this field>,
    
    	            "field": <entity field>,
    
    	            "type": <type of field>
    
    	        },
    
    	...
    
    	    ],
    
    	    "orderBy": <order by filter>,
    
    	    "filter": {
    
    	        <entity filter>
    
    	    }
    
    }

| \--| File report| Download a file-based report exporting a set of entities.  
**Example****Request**| | `/api/entity//export*`  
---  
 **Payload**`{``Â Â Â Â "exportFileType": "CSV",``Â Â Â Â "limit": 100,``Â Â Â Â "locale": "en",``Â Â Â Â "filename": "Alerts Page Report",``Â Â Â Â "separator": "|",``Â Â Â Â "filenameDateFormat": "dd-MM-yyyy HH'h'mm'm'ss's'",``Â Â Â Â "columnModels": [``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â {``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "title": "Bin",``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "field": "id",``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "type": "text"``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â },``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â {``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "title": "Brand",``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "field": "brand",``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "type": "text"``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â },``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â {``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "title": "Issuer",``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "field": "issuer",``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "type": "text"``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â }``Â Â Â Â ],``Â Â Â Â "orderBy": "brand asc",``Â Â Â Â "entityName": "binbase",``Â Â Â Â "filter": "'issuer' eq 'AMERICAN EXPRESS'"``}`  
---  
**Response**|  HTTP 200 â _application/[vnd.ms](http://vnd.ms)-excel_ Returns a file based report.  
  
### Custom Extensionsâ

**Synchronously execute a custom extension**

**Request**

Method| Endpoint| Payload| Parameters| Returns| Description  
---|---|---|---|---|---  
**POST**| 
    
    
    /api/extension/action

| **Payload**|  Contains a list of `ids`, an `entityType` that can be `event` or `case`, an `actionType` with the name of the extension and a `data` object for adding any custom parameters, to be parsed by the extension code. Because this endpoint executes the extension synchronously, `ids` may contain multiple identifiers. Please see the example below.  
---  
\--| Whether synchronous extension succeeded and associated label and data.| Execute a configured [custom extension](../../../configuration-guide/extension-points/custom-extensions/extensions-configuration) synchronously and get a response on whether its execution succeeded and additional label and data.  
**Example****Request**| | `/api/extension/action`  
---  
**Payload**`{``Â Â Â Â "ids": [,``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â {``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "id": "691ccac6-801c-4a6b-908a-c66fec06433e",``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "timestamp": "1565107133502",``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "channelId": "cmp"``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â },``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â {``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "id": "ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff",``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "timestamp": "1234567891234",``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "channelId": "legacy"``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â }``Â Â Â Â ],``Â Â Â Â "entityType": "EVENT",``Â Â Â Â "actionType": "myCustomExtension",``Â Â Â Â "data": {``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "myCustomKeyParam": "myCustomValue"``Â Â Â Â }``}`  
---  
**Response**|  HTTP 200 â Returns whether the synchronous extension succeeded, a custom label and associated data.`{`` "label": "Action executed",`` "data": "null",`` "succeeded": "true"``}`  
  
**Submit the asynchronous execution a custom extension**

**Request**

Method| Endpoint| Payload| Parameters| Returns| Description  
---|---|---|---|---|---  
**POST**| 
    
    
    /api/async-action/submit

| **Payload**|  Contains a list of `ids`, an `entityType` that can be `event` or `case`, an `actionType` with the name of the extension and a `data` object for adding any custom parameters, to be parsed by the extension code. Because this endpoint submits the extension to be executed asynchronously, `ids` may contain only one identifier (the identifier of the entity over which the extension will be executed on). Please see the example below.  
---  
\--| Whether asynchronous extension was successfully submitted for execution.| Submit the asynchronous execution of a configured [custom extension](../../../configuration-guide/extension-points/custom-extensions/extensions-configuration) and get a response on whether it was submitted successfully.  
**Example****Request**| | `/api/async-action/submit`  
---  
**Payload**`{``Â Â Â Â "ids": [,``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â {``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "id": "691ccac6-801c-4a6b-908a-c66fec06433e",``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "timestamp": "1565107133502",``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "channelId": "cmp"``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â }``Â Â Â Â ],``Â Â Â Â "entityType": "CASE",``Â Â Â Â "actionType": "myCustomExtension",``Â Â Â Â "data": {``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "myCustomKeyParam": "myCustomValue"``Â Â Â Â }``}`

**Response**|  HTTP 200 â Returns whether the extension was submitted successfully for asynchronous execution.`{`` "label": "The async action has been submitted for execution",`` "data": {``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "ids": [,``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â {``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "id": "691ccac6-801c-4a6b-908a-c66fec06433e",``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "timestamp": "1565107133502",``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "channelId": "cmp"``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â }``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â ],``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "entityType": "CASE",``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "actionType": "myCustomExtension",``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "data": {``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "myCustomKeyParam": "myCustomValue"``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â }``}`` "succeeded": "true"``}`  
  
**Check the status of the asynchronous execution of an extension**

**Request**

Method| Endpoint| Payload| Parameters| Returns| Description  
---|---|---|---|---|---  
**POST**| 
    
    
    /api/async-action/check

| **Payload**|  Contains a list of `ids`, an `entityType` that can be `event` or `case` and an `actionType` with the name of the extension. No `data` is required like in the `submit` endpoint. Because this endpoint checks the status of the execution of a custom extension, `ids` may contain only one identifier (the identifier of the entity over which the extension executes). Please see the example below.  
---  
\--| Status, amongst other information, of the execution of the given custom extension on the given entity.| Get the status of an asynchronous execution of a configured [custom extension](../../../configuration-guide/extension-points/custom-extensions/extensions-configuration).  
**Example****Request**| | `/api/async-action/submit`  
---  
**Payload**`{``Â Â Â Â "ids": [,``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â {``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "id": "691ccac6-801c-4a6b-908a-c66fec06433e",``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "timestamp": "1565107133502",``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â "channelId": "cmp"``Â Â Â Â Â Â Â Â }``Â Â Â Â "entityType": "CASE",``Â Â Â Â "actionType": "myCustomExtension",``}`  
---
